- **grill**: Standalone adversarial design-grill — the design-level mirror of `/review`. Runs by invocation between `/plan` and `/breakdown` to attack the FINISHED design (`plan.md` + its `spec.md`) before `/breakdown` spends effort decomposing it. Dispatches the `devils-advocate` adversary (which resolves a three-ring codebase blast radius and self-gated web-verification), cross-examines every attack with a non-author refutation pass (architect excluded), writes `specs/[feature]/grill.md`, and recommends a 4-way disposition (PROCEED / REVISE-PLAN / RE-ENTER-UPSTREAM / KILL). Opt-in — never an auto-gate. ([webmint/mintEnvoy](https://github.com/webmint/mintEnvoy/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/grill.md))

- **grillme**: Socratic plan validation. Grills your plan one question at a time against the project's CONTEXT.md glossary and prior ADRs; resolves terminology and decisions inline. Triggers: "grill my plan", "stress-test this plan", "validate this approach", "before I implement X", "is this consistent with our domain", "challenge my assumptions". - **ground-or-propose-metpo**: Deep-research the ungrounded growth-condition terms in the media recipes (atmosphere, pH, temperature, salinity, aeration, light); ground to METPO first (else PATO/ENVO/UO), and where no good term exists, draft a METPO proposal. METPO-maximizing, not METPO-forcing. ([CultureBotAI/CultureMech](https://github.com/CultureBotAI/CultureMech/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/ground-or-propose-metpo.md) · ★12)
- **ground-or-propose-metpo**: Deep-research the ungrounded causal-graph predicates/nodes; ground to METPO first (else RO/OBO), and where no good term exists, draft a METPO proposal. - **groundwork-adr**: Author an Architecture Decision Record. Captures the why behind a choice so future agents and humans do not have to re-derive it. Triggers on "write an ADR for...", "document why we chose...", "we just decided X, capture it", "create a decision record", "/gw adr". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/adr.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-apply**: Read a remediation plan produced by the `audit` command's interactive HTML report and apply the chosen fixes to the repo. Closes the loop between audit and action. Triggers on phrases like "apply this remediation", "run the audit fixes", "`/gw apply &lt;file&gt;`". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/apply.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-audit**: Score the repo's context engineering maturity across the five layers and produce an action plan. Use when the user asks "is my repo AI-ready?", "how good is our context setup?", "audit our CLAUDE.md", or before a major migration / handoff / new-hire onboarding. Also triggers for "/gw audit" and "context audit". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/audit.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-capture**: Turn subagent transcript capture on or off for this project. ([amitbaz/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/amitbaz/ai-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-capture.md))
- **groundwork-document**: Scan the codebase and produce or refresh CLAUDE.md / AGENTS.md / .cursor/rules/ with current, correct information. Triggers on "refresh CLAUDE.md", "update our context files", "document this codebase for the agent", "/gw document". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/document.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-drift**: Audit whether the project's context files are still true ([Exilitys/ExilitysSkills](https://github.com/Exilitys/ExilitysSkills/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-drift.md))
- **groundwork-hooks**: Install the contract gate and session-start hooks into this repo ([Exilitys/ExilitysSkills](https://github.com/Exilitys/ExilitysSkills/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-hooks.md))
- **groundwork-hunt**: Hunt a groundwork record for what is missing from it ([ananmouaz/groundwork](https://github.com/ananmouaz/groundwork/blob/HEAD/commands/groundwork-hunt.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-init**: Bootstrap a repo with the full context engineering scaffolding. Run this on a new repo or an existing repo that has no AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md/.cursor/rules/ yet. Triggers on phrases like "initialize this repo for AI", "set up context engineering", "bootstrap AGENTS.md", "make this AI-first", "/gw init". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/init.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-mcp**: Design a Model Context Protocol (MCP) configuration with least-privilege, human-in-the-loop, and auditing baked in. Use this before connecting an agent to a real system (database, deploy pipeline, API, file store). Triggers on "set up MCP for...", "give the agent access to...", "design an MCP server", "least privilege agent access", "/gw mcp". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/mcp.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-onboard**: Produce a tailored orientation brief that hands off a task to a fresh agent or engineer with the right context, no more. Surfaces the 3 to 5 most relevant ADRs, recent plans, area-specific conventions, verification commands, and any MCP policy that applies. Use when starting a new chat session on a familiar repo, when handing work to a teammate, when a fresh agent joins a long task, or when you want to "compact" a noisy session into a clean briefing. Triggers on "onboard a new agent to this", "brief me on this area", "what context does this task need", "/gw onboard", "handoff to fresh agent". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/onboard.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-plan**: Produce a Plan Mode contract before any non-trivial change. Names files touched, approach, verification commands, and rollback. Required for any change touching more than three files, any refactor, any public-API change, or any migration. Triggers on "plan this change", "draft a plan for...", "before I start coding...", "/gw plan", "/plan mode". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/plan.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-report**: Sweep for new subagent transcripts and report what the captured runs cost, by role. ([amitbaz/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/amitbaz/ai-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-report.md))
- **groundwork-scope**: Generate file-pattern-scoped rules so the right context loads for the right files. Triggers on "scope these rules", "per-directory rules", "frontend rules separate from backend", "/gw scope". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/scope.md) · ★1)
- **groundwork-stats**: Show whether transcript capture is on for this project and what has been captured so far. ([amitbaz/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/amitbaz/ai-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/groundwork/commands/groundwork-stats.md))
- **groundwork-verify**: Set up verification loops so the agent can self-correct. Triggers on "add verification", "wire up tests for the agent", "self-correcting loop", "/gw verify". ([IgniticAB/groundwork](https://github.com/IgniticAB/groundwork/blob/HEAD/skills/groundwork/commands/verify.md) · ★1)
